### 2026-09-18 17:57:58  #其他

【國內首個可輔助糖尿病治療決策的動態血糖儀獲批】9月18日，雅培宣佈，瞬感2代獲國家藥監局整合式持續葡萄糖監測系統（iCGM）批准，成為國內首個也是目前唯一獲此認證的動態血糖儀。瞬感2代早於2023年在國內獲批，身份是輔助式持續葡萄糖監測系統（CGM）。也就是，產品測量結果僅供使用者跟蹤糖值變化趨勢，不作為調整和決定治療方案的依據，資料亦無法進入臨床治療決策環節。當前按新標準獲批則意味著，其測量結果可直接用於成人糖尿病患者的治療輔助決策。截至目前，瞬感2代也是國內唯一可合規用於成人糖尿病治療輔助決策的持續葡萄糖監測系統產品。

### 2026-09-18 17:46:00  #公司 #國際

【Anthropic與OpenAI尋求小型資料中心交易】據知情人士，Anthropic和OpenAI正尋求規模較小的人工智慧資料中心交易，以加快算力部署。兩家公司過去一年已簽署多項數百兆瓦乃至吉瓦級大型資料中心協議，目前還在尋求20至30兆瓦規模的算力專案。訊息人士稱，Anthropic已在英國及北歐地區接洽相關合作，OpenAI也在北歐探索類似機會，雙方同時就美國市場的同等規模專案進行洽談。業內人士表示，小型算力專案的優勢在於可更快獲得可用容量，尤其適合分散式推理工作負載。相比大模型訓練需要大量晶片緊密協同，AI推理可分散至多個較小叢集。隨著人工智慧應用加速落地，資料中心算力需求正逐步由訓練向推理傾斜。JLL預計，到2027年，全球資料中心用於AI推理的容量佔比將超過訓練。

【澳大利亞擬在政府工作場所禁用智慧眼鏡】據路透社等多家媒體17日報道，考慮到隱私和安全等因素，澳大利亞擬禁止在聯邦政府工作場所使用配備攝像頭的智慧眼鏡。澳大利亞公共服務部長凱蒂·加拉格說，智慧眼鏡可能會引發隱私和安全擔憂，特別是它們能記錄和捕獲資訊，因而“需要為公務員提供明確和一貫的指導”。加拉格已要求公共服務委員會就在聯邦政府工作場所使用智慧眼鏡提出規範建議，包括是否應全面禁止以及哪些情形可能是必要例外。此外，澳政府還將召集包括微軟、澳大利亞聯邦銀行、澳大利亞電信等多家在澳大型企業舉行圓桌會議，圍繞工作場所人工智慧的使用規範進行磋商。澳大利亞眼下已在部分場所限制智慧眼鏡等裝置的使用。布里斯班市議會近期禁止在公共泳池使用智慧眼鏡及類似可穿戴裝置隱蔽錄製，托兒場所也限制使用配備攝像頭的個人裝置。（新華社）

### 2026-09-18 17:16:37  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro Area #Construction Output #Importance 1

Eurozone Construction Output Falls the Most in 5 Months — Eurozone construction output dropped by 2% year-on-year in July 2026, the steepest decline since February, following an upwardly revised 1.4% decrease in June. Building activity continued to contract (-6.4% vs -7.4% in June), while specialised construction activities also remained under pressure (-1.6% vs -0.4%). Meanwhile, civil engineering rose by 0.6%, after being flat in the previous month. Among major economies, output fell in Spain (-9.7%), France (-5%) and Italy (-1.2%), but increased in Germany (1%). On a monthly basis, construction output was flat, after an upwardly revised 1.5% slump in June. In the broader European Union, construction activity shrank by 1.8% on a yearly basis and fell by 0.3% from the previous month.

### 2026-09-18 17:11:53  #Trading Economics #Economy #Bulgaria #Current Account #Importance 1

Bulgaria Current Account Deficit Narrows in July — Bulgaria's current account deficit narrowed to EUR 269.0 million in July 2026 from EUR 303.7 million in the same month a year earlier. The improvement came despite a wider goods deficit of EUR 853.1 million, compared with EUR 752.4 million, as the primary income deficit narrowed to EUR 532.0 million from EUR 617.4 million and the secondary income surplus increased to EUR 240.8 million from EUR 149.9 million. Meanwhile, the services surplus edged down to EUR 875.2 million from EUR 916.2 million, leaving the combined goods and services balance at a surplus of EUR 22.2 million, down from EUR 163.8 million. In January-July 2026, the current account recorded a deficit of EUR 5,426.8 million, compared with a deficit of EUR 2,470.6 million in the corresponding period of 2025.

Lithium Falls to 8-Month Low — Lithium carbonate prices in China fell under CNY 135,000 per tonne in September, the lowest year-to-date, after an upward revision to stockpiles drove markets to price a more comfortable supply backdrop. Industry group SMM changed their methodology to compile inventory data that prompted a 175,000-ton increase in Chinese stockpiles, double the earlier estimates. This was combined with a strong outlook for Australian producers. Mineral Resources restarted Bald Hill lithium mine following an 18-month suspension, while Core Lithium restarted its Finniss project. A sharper pullback was prevented by more disruption in CATL's Jianxiawo mine, China's largest by capacity and responsible for 4% of global supply. Chinese authorities revoked their environmental approvals for the mine to extend the period of permit hurdles for over one year, coinciding with Beijing's anti-involution campaign and efforts to combat producer overcapacity.

### 2026-09-18 16:52:47  #Trading Economics #Economy #Macau #Tourist Arrivals #Importance 1

Macau Tourist Arrivals Climb to Record High — The number of tourist arrivals in Macau rose by 6.1% year-on-year to 4.478 million in August 2026, following a 2.4% gain in the previous month. This marked the highest number of arrivals on record, as same-day visitors jumped by 11.7% to 2.853 million, while overnight visitors fell by 2.4% to 1.625 million. In addition, the average length of stay shortened by 0.1 day from a year earlier to 1.0 day, while the average stay of overnight visitors remained unchanged at 2.3 days. By source market, arrivals from China increased by 9.3% to 3.562 million, with visitors traveling under the Individual Visit Scheme rising by 12.7% to 2.020 million. Arrivals from Taiwan (101,282) and Malaysia (8,161) also increased by 13.7% and 2.1%, respectively. In contrast, visitor arrivals from Hong Kong (643,445), the Philippines (31,282), and Indonesia (11,106) declined by 5.6%, 18.5%, and 16.9%, respectively. In the January–August period, total visitor arrivals rose by 7.7% to 28.964 million.

### 2026-09-18 16:42:53  #Trading Economics #Markets #Turkey #Stock Market #Importance 1

BIST 100 Plunges on Fund Liquidations — The Turkish BIST 100 index sank over 8% to 13,300 since testing three-month highs at 14,500 in early September, despite a lower lira, as a run on investment funds spread into equity prices of major Turkish companies. Leading Istanbul fund manager Pusula Portfoy announced that some of its funds could not meet redemption requests, leading investors across the country to liquidate positions in similar asset classes. The run triggered a crisis on funds that had been accused artificially inflating the NAVs through co-ordinated buying of assets with limited outstanding shares, driving authorities to start the liquidation process of major firms in the Turkish financial system, including Hedef, Tera, Atlas, A1, and Pardus Portfoy. The Turkish central bank had held interest rates unchanged this month but eased collateral conditions on short-term funding markets to prop-up liquidity in financial stress.

### 2026-09-18 16:39:42  #Trading Economics #Markets #Switzerland #Currency #Importance 0

Swiss Franc Weakens to Over One-Year Low — The Swiss franc weakened past 0.82 per USD, hitting its lowest level since May of 2025, on a widening interest rate differential with the US and selling pressure from new carry trades. The Federal Reserve's first rate hike in three years boosted greenback demand, while the Bank of Japan's policy tightening and a historic Washington–Tokyo intervention to support the yen reduced the currency's appeal as a carry-trade funding source. Traders pivoted to alternatives offering exchange-rate stability and low borrowing costs, such as the Swiss franc, selling franc loans for higher-yielding assets and pressuring the currency lower. Contrasting with other central banks, the Swiss National Bank is expected to hold its policy rate at 0% through year-end, the lowest among major economies, favoring interventions to cap excessive franc strength. Offsetting this downward pressure, persistent Middle East tensions threatening energy supplies lift safe-haven demand.

Poland Producer Inflation Highest Since 2023 — Annual producer inflation in Poland accelerated to 4.2% in August 2026 from an upwardly revised 3.1% in the previous month, exceeding market expectations of 3.6%. It marked the sixth consecutive month of producer price growth and the highest reading since April 2023, as prices rose at a faster pace in mining and quarrying (6.4% vs 4% in July), manufacturing (4.6% vs 3.5%), and electricity, gas, steam and hot water supply (1.4% vs 0.3%). On the other hand, price growth eased in water supply; sewerage, waste management and remediation activities (2.2% vs 2.3%). On a monthly basis, producer prices rose 0.6%, slowing from an upwardly revised 1% in the previous month. For the January-August period, producer prices increased 1.3%.

Eurozone Current Account Surplus Widens in July — The eurozone current account surplus widened to €36.5 billion in July 2026, up from €29.7 billion a year earlier, driven primarily by stronger goods and services balances. The goods surplus rose to €39.8 billion from €31.8 billion, while the services surplus increased to €19.3 billion from €15.1 billion. Partially offsetting the gains, the primary income deficit widened to €4.4 billion from €2.0 billion, while the secondary income deficit increased to €18.1 billion from €15.2 billion. For the first seven months of the year, the current account surplus reached €151.1 billion, up from €131.6 billion over the same period in 2025.

Euro Nears July Low as Markets Scale Back ECB Rate-Hike Bets — The euro traded just below $1.15, near its weakest level since late July, and was down nearly 1% against the US dollar as traders scaled back expectations for further ECB rate hikes. Some analysts argued that markets had gone too far in pricing additional ECB hikes, noting that higher energy prices could weigh on growth and ultimately ease inflationary pressures. ### 2026-09-18 16:12:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro area #Inflation Expectations #Importance 2

Euro Area Consumer Inflation Expectations Edge Up — Median expectations for price growth over the next 12 months in the Euro Area increased to 3% in August 2026 from 2.9%. Expectations for inflation three years ahead also increased, to 2.9% from 2.7%, while the five-year outlook went up to 2.5% from 2.4%. Uncertainty about expectations for inflation over the next 12 months decreased, but remained higher than the level prevailing before the start of the conflict in the Middle East. Lower-income households continued to perceive and anticipate higher inflation than higher-income groups, while younger consumers maintained more moderate expectations than older respondents. Also, expectations for nominal income growth were unchanged at 1%. Those for economic growth remained at -1.2% while expectations for the unemployment rate 12 months ahead decreased to 11% from 11.2%. In addition, consumers expected the price of their home to increase by 3.4% over the next 12 months and mortgage interest rates to increase 4.9%, the same as in July.

Yen Remains Volatile as Ueda Reaffirms Tightening Path — The Japanese yen weakened past 157 per dollar on Friday, hitting two-week lows after the Bank of Japan raised interest rates in a widely telegraphed move, with two officials dissenting to the decision. The currency later pared its decline after Governor Kazuo Ueda said at the post-meeting press conference that the BOJ remains committed to raising rates and adjusting the extent of monetary accommodation based on changes in economic activity, prices and financial conditions. The central bank lifted its policy rate by 25 basis points to 1.25%, the highest since April 1995, as policymakers contend with persistent inflation and disruptions stemming from the Middle East. The hike was opposed by board members Toichiro Asada and Ayano Sato, highlighting some resistance to a quicker tightening cycle. Meanwhile, Japan’s core inflation eased to 1.7% in August from 1.8% in July, marking its first decline in four months.

【華源證券：首予濰柴動力“買入”評級，AI電力時代公司價值重塑】華源證券研報指出，濰柴動力柴發+氣發+SOFC三輪驅動，AI電力時代公司價值重塑。2020年至今，公司多次重新整理商業化柴油機本體熱效率世界紀錄。2025年M系列大缸徑發動機全年銷量首次突破1萬臺，推出全球首款5兆瓦級高速柴油發電產品20M61。當前公司業務已涵蓋動力系統、商用車、農業裝備、智慧物流、電力能源等多個業務板塊。2026-2030年燃機用於滿足AIDC用電需求的有效供給累計在100GW左右，故非燃機AIDC用電需求缺口預計也在100GW以上，以往復式內燃機/SOFC為代表的其他離網發電形式或大有可為。往復式內燃機在AI發電的應用主要是柴發（備電）與氣發，濰柴產品具備全球競爭力。SOFC可能是中長期最具潛力的離網主電形式，濰柴深度佈局金屬支撐路線。公司作為國內柴發/氣發/SOFC均有較深度佈局的頭部企業，有望充分受益於AI電力需求的持續景氣，首次覆蓋，給予“買入”評級。

### 2026-09-18 15:12:04  #Trading Economics #Economy #Slovakia #Current Account #Importance 1

Slovakia Current Account Deficit Widens in July — Slovakia's current account deficit widened to EUR 1,035.3 million in July 2026 from EUR 575.8 million in the corresponding month of the previous year. The goods account deficit widened to EUR 523.7 million from EUR 204.6 million, while the primary income shortfall increased to EUR 420.8 million from EUR 265.8 million. In contrast, the services surplus increased to EUR 39.9 million from EUR 30.5 million, while the secondary income deficit narrowed to EUR 130.6 million from EUR 136.0 million. In the first seven months of the year, the country's current account deficit totaled EUR 2,418.5 million, down from EUR 3,275.3 million in the corresponding period of 2025.

【比亞迪汽車工業有限公司、比亞迪汽車有限公司召回部分國產汽車】日前，比亞迪汽車工業有限公司、比亞迪汽車有限公司根據《缺陷汽車產品召回管理條例》《缺陷汽車產品召回管理條例實施辦法》的要求，向國家市場監督管理總局備案了召回計劃。本次召回範圍內的部分車輛，由於生產製造問題，導致制動踏板限位墊材料批次性異常，長時間使用後，限位墊可能開裂破損。極端情況下，限位墊脫落，造成制動踏板未被踩下時制動燈常亮，向後方傳遞錯誤訊號，存在安全隱患。比亞迪汽車工業有限公司、比亞迪汽車有限公司將委託授權經銷商，為召回範圍內的車輛免費更換改善後的限位墊，以消除安全隱患。因部分車輛此前已更換改善後的限位墊，本次召回此部分車輛無需再次更換。（市場監管總局）

【寶馬（中國）汽車貿易有限公司召回部分進口S1000RR型摩托車】日前，寶馬（中國）汽車貿易有限公司根據《缺陷汽車產品召回管理條例》《缺陷汽車產品召回管理條例實施辦法》的要求，向國家市場監督管理總局備案了召回計劃。召回編號S2026M0118V：自即日起，召回2020年10月27日至2026年4月7日期間生產的部分進口S1000RR型摩托車，共計5593輛。本次召回範圍內的摩托車，由於設計原因，在特定工況下，點火鎖內部元件可能受振動影響發生磨損，造成電氣接觸不良，點火缺火。若出現點火中斷，導致發動機熄火，車燈、組合儀表及各類輔助系統等電氣功能全部失效，存在安全隱患。寶馬（中國）汽車貿易有限公司將委託授權經銷商為召回範圍內的車輛免費進行軟體升級，增加兩級警告機制，並對行駛里程大於1500公里的車輛在軟體升級後進行功能測試，若存在相應故障記錄則更換點火鎖，以消除安全隱患。

### 2026-09-18 15:06:39  #Trading Economics #Markets #Japan #Currency #Importance 1

Japanese Shares Rise After BOJ Decision — The Nikkei 225 Index advanced 1.38% to finish at 65,019 on Friday, extending its winning streak to three sessions after the Bank of Japan raised its policy rate by 25 basis points to 1.25% in a widely telegraphed move. The decision was not unanimous, however, as board members Toichiro Asada and Ayano Sato opposed the increase, signaling some resistance to a more rapid pace of policy tightening. Despite the dissent, Governor Kazuo Ueda said at the post-meeting press conference that the BOJ remains prepared to raise rates further and adjust the level of monetary easing as economic conditions evolve. Meanwhile, official data showed Japan’s core inflation slowing to 1.7% in August from 1.8% in July, its first decline in four months. Technology and AI-related shares led the market higher after similar gains on Wall Street overnight, with Kioxia (9.4%), SoftBank Group (1.1%), Advantest (6%), Lasertec (8.7%), and Taiyo Yuden (4.2%) among the notable advancers.

### 2026-09-18 15:02:53  #其他

【範波會見羅氏診斷全球運營總裁瑞秋·克萊因一行】9月18日，蘇州市委書記範波會見了羅氏診斷全球運營總裁瑞秋·克萊因一行。範波代表市委、市政府對羅氏給予蘇州發展的關心支援表示感謝。他說，蘇州是中國東部地區重要的中心城市，當前正深入學習貫徹習近平總書記就發展先進製造業作出的重要指示精神和全國先進製造業大會部署，強化創新驅動，因地制宜發展新質生產力，最佳化完善“1030”產業體系，縱深開展“人工智慧+製造”行動，建設具有國際競爭力的“智慧製造之城”。生物醫藥及高階醫療器械是蘇州加力佈局的十大重點新興產業之一，羅氏是全球領先的生物醫藥企業，雙方合作基礎深厚、深化合作空間廣闊。希望羅氏持續加大在蘇投資力度，積極匯入更多優質專案、創新技術、先進理念，助力蘇州推動生物醫藥全產業鏈開放創新，共同書寫互利共贏新篇章。蘇州將積極擴大高水平對外開放，深入開展“三服務”專項行動，努力打造“有呼必應、事事閉環”營商環境品牌，更好服務廣大企業紮根蘇州、創新發展。（蘇州日報）

### 2026-09-18 14:55:46  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

UK Natural Gas Prices Rebound — UK natural gas prices rose toward 193 pence per therm on Friday, rebounding after three straight sessions of declines, with depleted reserves and disruptions to LNG shipments remaining in focus. The region is entering winter with low storage levels after the US-Iran war severely curtailed LNG supplies from the Persian Gulf and record summer heatwaves hampered efforts to rebuild inventories. Underground storage facilities were only around 68% full, well below seasonal averages and the EU’s target of 80% capacity by December. Strong demand from Asia and other buyers could also drive more intense competition for LNG cargoes when colder weather sets in, leaving the region more exposed to price spikes. Despite Friday’s gain, UK gas prices have fallen more than 2% this week, their first weekly decline in six weeks.

ASX 200 Weekly Decline Extends to 3rd Straight Period — The ASX 200 finished flat at 8,731 on Friday, following recent gains, with strength in tech, industrial services, and non-energy minerals offset by weakness in healthcare, energy names, and consumer non-durables. Early gains faded after Reserve Bank of Australia's Governor Michele Bullock struck a hawkish tone in parliament, warning inflation remains stubbornly high and upside risks are materializing, keeping the door open for further hikes. Meanwhile, U.S. stock futures firmed on softer oil prices and U.S. Treasury yields. Heavyweight BHP Group climbed 1.4%, while gold miners Evolution Mining (4.4%) and Northern Star Resources (2.4%) also advanced. In contrast, the big four banks slipped between 0.2% and 1.9%, with Xero (-4.1%) and Bluescope (-3.1%) also lagging. For the week, the benchmark index eased 0.1%, its third straight decline, as caution grew ahead of Australia's labor data for August, due next week, after July’s surprise rise in unemployment and drop in jobs.

【Russia ramps use of modified RM48U training missiles, may be preserving precision stockpiles for winter offensive】Ukrainian official data show Russia has used large numbers of modified training missiles (RM48U), low‑precision weapons originally for air‑defense target practice, driving July–early August launches to record levels. Of 228 ballistic and hypersonic missiles fired in that period, RM48U accounted for more than half, a deployment scale not previously reported that helps explain Moscow’s ability to expand summer strikes without rapidly depleting Iskander and other precision attack missiles. RUSI senior researcher Sidharth Kaushal said mass RM48U launches can exhaust Ukraine’s interceptor stocks and hit lower‑value targets while conserving advanced missiles—Iskander, KN‑23 and Zircon—for a possible intensified winter ballistic campaign. Ukrainian tallies also show Iskander launches fell from over 100 the prior month to 38 as RM48U strikes surged in July.

### 2026-09-18 14:48:42  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

European Gas Prices Bounce Back — European natural gas prices rose above €77/MWh on Friday, rebounding after three consecutive sessions of losses, with depleted reserves and disruptions to LNG shipments remaining in focus. The region is entering winter with low storage levels after the US-Iran war severely curtailed LNG supplies from the Persian Gulf and record summer heatwaves hampered efforts to rebuild inventories. Underground storage facilities were only around 68% full, well below seasonal averages and the EU’s target of 80% capacity by December. Strong demand from Asia and other buyers could also drive more intense competition for LNG cargoes when colder weather sets in, leaving the region more exposed to price spikes. European gas prices have nearly tripled so far this year and reached their highest level since December 2022 earlier this week.

【平安證券：首予平高電氣“推薦”評級，特高壓龍頭再迎成長週期】平安證券研報指出，平高電氣電網投資景氣上行，特高壓龍頭再迎成長週期。2020—2025年，公司高壓板塊收入佔比由42.4%提升至62.3%，板塊毛利率由15.07%提升至27.76%，業務結構持續向高附加值產品傾斜，推動利潤釋放快於收入增長。電網投資進入補強週期，主網、特高壓及海內外需求持續擴容。特高壓專案形成“十四五結轉兌現、十五五新增接力”的連續梯隊，疊加煤電、核電等大型電源建設以及海外電網擴容與更新需求，高壓開關行業景氣有望延續。產品結構最佳化與內部提質增效共同推動盈利能力持續改善。海外市場：需求多點開花，競爭格局更優，公司有望逐步出海拓展。公司作為國內特高壓GIS核心供應商，有望充分受益於“十五五”電網資本開支擴張；疊加高壓業務佔比提升、在手訂單逐步交付和內部提質增效，預計盈利能力繼續改善，首次覆蓋，給予“推薦”評級。

European Stocks Set for Cautious Open — European equity markets were poised for a subdued start on Friday after posting gains for two consecutive sessions, with investors weighing the latest decisions from major central banks alongside falling oil prices. On Thursday, the Bank of England left interest rates unchanged but cautioned that a prolonged Middle East conflict could force it to tighten policy, while the Federal Reserve and Bank of Japan both raised rates this week. Oil prices, meanwhile, extended their decline for a third session as Saudi Arabia worked to restore flows through its East-West pipeline, while President Donald Trump is due to meet with Gulf leaders next week. European markets will also focus on fresh economic data, including Germany’s PPI, UK retail sales and the Eurozone current account, for clues on the region’s economic outlook. In premarket trading, Euro Stoxx 50 and Stoxx 600 futures were little changed.

【Multiple private banks raise deposit rates】After a period of cuts and product withdrawals under net interest margin pressure, several private banks have begun lifting select deposit rates. WeBank raised its 3‑year fixed deposit rate to 1.75% from 1.60% (+15bps). WeBank’s current on‑term retail deposit rates are: 5y 1.60%, 3y 1.75%, 2y 1.60%, 1y 1.50%, 6m 1.30%, 3m 1.10% — with a 3y/5y inversion. 【交易員聚焦儲氣補庫，歐洲天然氣價格上漲】距離供暖季開啟僅剩數週，交易員關注儲氣庫補庫工作，歐洲天然氣價格走高。基準期貨週五盤中最大漲幅達2.8%，此前多個交易日隨原油一同下跌。儘管美伊外交斡旋仍在推進，但儲氣庫庫存異常偏低仍是天然氣市場的核心主線。德國計劃大幅擴容現有招標專案，向供應商支付費用以保障冬季天然氣供應，以此激勵交易商填充本國儲氣設施。全球天然氣供應依舊緊張，歐洲加大采購的任何訊號都將支撐價格。自衝突爆發以來，歐洲天然氣期貨價格已上漲一倍有餘，本月早些時候觸及2022年末以來高點。中東持續衝突切斷了全球約五分之一的液化天然氣供應，幾乎沒有跡象表明霍爾木茲海峽的液化天然氣運輸會很快恢復。

【日本央行缺乏加息緊迫感，助推套息交易】本次日本央行會議對日元並非利好，全球套息交易再度具備實施條件。儘管央行落實了美國財長斯科特·貝森特所呼籲、且交易員早已計價的政策收緊，但多項因素利空日元：此次加息決議遭到兩名政策委員反對，無人主張更大幅度加息；央行宣告雖承諾將持續加息，卻未給出加息時間節點，未能釋放緊迫的加息訊號。日本基準利率仍遠低於中性利率水平，若不連續加息，日元弱勢格局將會延續。在十國集團經濟體中，目前僅有瑞士法郎利率更低，瑞士央行將於下週召開會議。倘若瑞士央行重申維持零利率的傾向，兩大主要融資貨幣對應的匯率波動將會維持低位。

### 2026-09-18 14:08:41  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Retail Sales MoM #Importance 2

UK Retail Sales Unexpectedly Rise in August — UK retail sales volumes rose 0.5% month-on-month in August 2026, defying market expectations of a 0.2% fall and rebounding from a 0.5% decline in July. Non-store retailers partially recovered from July’s decline, when earlier promotional activity in June pulled sales forward. Non-food store sales volumes rose 0.6%, with department stores recovering from stock availability issues and clothing retailers rebounding after weaker July sales. Food stores also performed well over the three months to August, with supermarkets reporting strong sales in July and August. Retailers selling alcohol and beverages cited promotions, hot weather and the World Cup as factors supporting sales. Meanwhile, fuel sales fell over the month as prices rose sharply, with retailers reporting that consumers were changing their habits, including only partially filling their tanks.

### 2026-09-18 14:05:55  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Producer Prices Change #Importance 2

German Producer Inflation Hits Over 3-Year High — Germany’s producer prices advanced 4.6% year-on-year in August 2026, marking the fifth straight month of producer price growth and accelerating from July’s reading of 3.0% and surpassing market forecasts of 4.1%. It was the fastest increase in producer prices since April 2023, mainly boosted by rises in energy prices (8.3% vs 3.8%). Meanwhile, prices for intermediate goods accelerated to 6.1% from 5.4% in July. Excluding energy, producer prices rose 3.1%. Monthly, prices rose 1.1%, the same pace as in July and still the fastest pace in three months, exceeding market estimates for a milder 0.4% increase.

New Zealand Stocks End Week 1.1% Higher — The NZX 50 lost 17 points, or 0.1%, to close at 13,739 on Friday, reversing its morning gains and halting a two-session winning streak, as traders continued to assess the GDP data. Thursday’s data showed New Zealand’s GDP eased to 0.1% quarterly from 0.2%. Traders anticipated China’s interest rate decision and US PMI data next week for clues about the economic outlook in the two countries. However, the latest data capped gains, as New Zealand’s trade deficit narrowed in August from July, while food inflation stayed at a 19-month low. A rally on Wall Street Thursday also helped prevent the index from falling deeper, as easing oil prices softened Treasury yields. Consumer staples, financials, and communication services mainly weighed on the index, with the biggest laggards including A2 Milk (-3.9%), Skellerup Holdings (-2.9%), Gentrack Group (-2.6%), Infratil (-1.0%), and Westpac Banking Corp (-0.9%). For the week, the index rose 1.1%, after falling in the previous week.

Rupiah Rises But Set for First Weekly Decline in 7 Weeks — The Indonesian rupiah edged higher to below IDR 17,750 per U.S. dollar on Friday, snapping a six-session losing streak as oil prices retreated further, easing concerns over imported inflation. Meanwhile, policymakers continued their efforts to contain food-price volatility after headline inflation accelerated to 3.19% in August. Globally, the dollar index steadied after reaching a seven-week high, with traders assessing the Federal Reserve’s policy outlook following its latest rate decision. For the week, however, the rupiah remains on track for a decline of around 0.7%, which would halt a six-week winning streak. Sentiment was pressured by an uncertain policy outlook, as Bank Indonesia seeks to maintain an adequate interest-rate differential with the U.S. while supporting rupiah stability and economic growth. The central bank will hold its policy meeting next week after leaving borrowing costs unchanged for a second straight month in August.

【ECB vice president VUJCIC: global bond yields at high levels do not threaten financial stability】ECB Vice President VUJCIC said market pricing for further ECB rate hikes has been driven mainly by rising energy prices, but policymakers will judge next moves on broader economic indicators rather than energy alone. He warned that if inf stays elevated through the autumn and hits household incomes and consumption it would weigh on GDP; the ECB will monitor incoming months and adjust policy accordingly. Global bond yields have risen to pre-financial-crisis levels amid higher inf and rate expectations plus large government and tech-sector issuance. VUJCIC said those moves do not pose financial-stability risks because euro-area banks are well capitalized and liquidity is ample.

### 2026-09-18 13:37:30  #公司

【上交所對ST中路及有關責任人予以通報批評，對其關聯方及責任人監管警示】9月17日，上海證券交易所釋出《關於對中路股份有限公司關聯方及有關責任人予以監管警示的決定》和《關於對中路股份有限公司及其實際控制人陳榮、控股股東上海中路（集團）有限公司和有關責任人予以通報批評的決定》。經查明，中路股份有限公司、實際控制人陳榮、控股股東上海中路（集團）有限公司在資訊披露、規範運作方面，有關責任人在職責履行方面，存在如下違規行為。中路優勢（天津）股權投資基金合夥企業（有限合夥）是中路股份出資99%並控股的企業。陳榮是中路股份、中路集團、上海中路投資管理中心（有限合夥）和上海機商實業有限公司的實際控制人。2024年2月至2025年6月，陳榮、中路集團、中路投資和上海機商等關聯方，透過中路優勢對外投資、支付諮詢顧問費等方式非經營性佔用上市公司資金1248萬元。公司未審議、未及時披露上述關聯方非經營性資金佔用構成的關聯交易事項，也未在2024年半年度報告、2024年年度報告和2025年半年度報告中披露上述關聯方非經營性佔用資金情況。2025年6月5日，公司披露《關於對上海證券交易所監管工作函的回覆公告》，其中明確公司2024年度前十大客戶、前十大供應商的名稱、交易金額、交易內容等具體情況，同時披露未發現公司2024年度主要客戶與供應商之間存在其他關聯關係或潛在利益安排，主要客戶及主要供應商與公司發生的交易具有商業實質。公司《回覆公告》中披露的部分客戶與供應商的關係、交易金額等與實際情況不符，資訊披露不準確。上交所決定，對中路股份有限公司關聯方上海中路投資管理中心（有限合夥）、上海機商實業有限公司、時任董事會秘書朱智予以監管警示。對中路股份有限公司及其實際控制人陳榮，控股股東上海中路（集團）有限公司，時任董事長、總經理陳閃予以通報批評。

【中美外長這通電話釋放出的資訊】根據2026年5月美國總統特朗普訪華時的雙方共識，習近平主席將於今年秋季對美國進行國事訪問。值此之際，中共中央政治局委員、外交部長王毅9月17日同美國國務卿魯比奧通電話。 從中方通稿看，這通電話直接點明核心問題：雙方“著重就兩國高層交往進行了深入討論”，並就中東局勢等問題交換了意見。這實際上透露出當前中美關係幾個值得重視的動向：一是，中美正在為下一階段高層互動作準備；二是，雙方都需要避免關係失序；三是，臺灣問題雖然沒有出現在通稿文字中，卻依然是決定中美關係能否穩定前行的深層變數。 一通電話無法解決中美之間長期積累的結構性矛盾，但可以校準預期、劃明邊界、為新一輪高層會晤開啟溝通空間。對於中美這樣兩個大國而言，這種溝通本身是十分必要的。（環球時報）

### 2026-09-18 13:05:15  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Palm Oil Retreats on Profit Taking But Heads for weekly gain — Malaysian palm oil futures hovered below MYR 4,910 per tonne, reversing recent gains as weakness in rival edible oils on Dalian and Chicago markets weighed on sentiment. Prices also pulled back from a 21-month high on profit-taking, while sluggish exports added pressure, with cargo surveyors estimating September 1–15 palm oil shipments fell between 17.8% and 25.6% from August. Crude oil also eased as extra cargoes tempered supply concerns, reducing palm’s biodiesel appeal. Even so, futures were still on track for a weekly gain of about 1.9%, supported by expectations of tighter supplies ahead. Indonesia’s B50 mandate is set to divert more palm oil to domestic use, while El Niño risks could curb output in both Indonesia and Malaysia. In India, imports rose 7% in August to 782,761 tonnes, the highest since February, as refiners replenished stocks ahead of the festival season. Separately, Malaysia raised its October crude palm oil reference price but kept the export duty unchanged at 10%.

Sensex Gains as Oil Prices Ease — India’s BSE Sensex edged up about 0.2% to 74,435 on Friday, after the benchmark was largely muted in the previous session, as investors took some comfort from easing crude oil prices, while continued Middle East tensions and a rush of initial public offerings capped gains. Expected foreign inflows linked to the IPO could support the rupee, although the heavy IPO pipeline is also drawing liquidity away from the secondary market. Among stocks, Lodha (3.5%), Yatharth (6.6%), Netweb (1.6%), and Syrma SGS (3.6%) were among the notable gainers. Meanwhile, tech stocks remained in the red, led by Infosys (-1.2%), Tech Mahindra (-1.9%), Coforge (-1.2%), and Wipro (-1.1%). Malaysia Food Prices Rise the Most in 11 Months — Food prices in Malaysia rose 1.9% year-on-year in August 2026, up slightly from a 1.8% increase in the previous month and marking the fastest pace since September 2025. Upward price pressures came from all components, including food consumed at home (1.4% vs 1.2% in July), cereals & cereal products (0.2% vs 0.2%), meat (3.5% vs 3.2%), fish & other seafood (0.9% vs 1.1%), milk, dairy products & eggs (1.5% vs 0.8%), oils & fats (0.1% vs 0.2%), fruits & nuts (1.4% vs 1.1%), vegetables (2.8% vs 1.0%), sugar, confectionery & desserts (0.5% vs 0.4% vs 0.5%), and food away from home (2.5% vs 2.5%).

### 2026-09-18 12:21:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Malaysia #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Malaysia Inflation Inches Up to 1.9% in August — Malaysia’s annual inflation rate edged up to 1.9% in August 2026, above the 1.8% market forecast and July’s four-month low, while remaining comfortably within Bank Negara’s 1-1/2%-2-1/2% target range. Price growth accelerated for food and beverages (1.9% vs 1.8% in July), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (2.8% vs 2.7%), housing (2.1% vs 1.8%), furnishings and household equipment (0.4% vs 0.3%), health (1.3% vs 1.1%), transport (2.0% vs 1.4%), restaurants and accommodation services (2.1% vs 2.0%), and miscellaneous goods and services (3.2% vs 2.9%). In the meantime, clothing inflation was unchanged at 0.1%, while cost eased for information and communication (2.6% vs 3.4%), recreation and culture (1.1% vs 1.2%), education (1.9% vs 2.0%), and insurance and financial services (1.0% vs 1.1%). Core inflation fell to 1.7%, its lowest since December 2024, from 1.8% in July. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.3%, rebounding from July’s flat reading.

### 2026-09-18 12:20:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #Malaysia #Exports YoY #Importance 1

Malaysia August Export Growth Tops Forecasts — Malaysia’s exports surged 45.5% year-on-year to MYR 191.1 billion in August 2026, accelerating from a 38% growth in both July and market expectations. Growth was mainly boosted by higher shipments of manufactured goods, which climbed 50.4% and accounted for 89.5% of total exports, particularly in electrical and electronic products (66.5%). Exports of mining products also soared 43.3%, due to increased shipments of tin (180.6%), metalliferous ores and metal scrap (76.1%), and liquefied natural gas (51.8%). However, the increase was partially offset by a decline in crude petroleum exports (-19.3%). By contrast, agricultural exports plunged 21.4%, reflecting lower sales of palm oil and palm-based products (-25%) and sawlog (-37.9%). Exports to all ten major destinations registered growth, led by the US (130.4%), Singapore (58.5%), China (19.9%), and the EU (20%). For January–August, exports totaled MYR 1,356.2 billion, up 31.2% from the same period last year.

### 2026-09-18 12:18:06  #Trading Economics #Economy #Malaysia #Imports YoY #Importance 1

Malaysia Imports Beat Expectations — Malaysia’s imports surged 41.1% year-on-year to MYR 163 billion in August 2026, surpassing market expectations of 33.3% and accelerating from 36.4% in July. The latest reading marked the twelfth consecutive month of annual growth, underscoring resilient domestic demand. Import growth was driven primarily by the manufacturing sector, where purchases rose 43.2%, led by the manufacture of electrical and electronic products (89.3%), machinery, equipment and parts (22%), and petroleum products (26.6%). Imports for the mining sector also climbed sharply (60.1%), supported by a strong increase in crude petroleum purchases (87%). In contrast, imports declined for agriculture (-2.9%) and other sectors (-5.9%). By trading partner, imports recorded the strongest increases from China (61.3%), followed by Taiwan (68.9%), Singapore (59.1%), and the US (3.2%). For the January-August period, total imports reached MYR 1,157.6 billion, from MYR 946.1 billion in the corresponding period a year earlier.

Yen Weakens as BOJ Hikes Rates — The Japanese yen weakened to around 157 per dollar on Friday, touching two-week lows after the Bank of Japan delivered a widely anticipated interest-rate hike. The central bank lifted its policy rate by 25 basis points to 1.25%, its highest level since April 1995, as policymakers contend with persistent inflationary pressures and ongoing disruptions from the Middle East. The decision was not unanimous, however, with board members Toichiro Asada and Ayano Sato voting against the increase. Attention now turns to the BOJ’s guidance on the scope and timing of further policy tightening. Analysts have indicated that Japan’s tightening cycle may struggle to match the pace of Federal Reserve rate increases, following the Fed’s hawkish hike earlier this week. Meanwhile, Japan’s core inflation slowed to 1.7% in August from 1.8% in July, marking its first decline in four months.

### 2026-09-18 12:06:40  #Trading Economics #Economy #Malaysia #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Malaysia Trade Surplus Beats Forecasts — Malaysia’s trade surplus stood at MYR 28.1 billion in August 2026, up from MYR 15.9 billion in the same month a year earlier and surpassing market expectations of MYR 25.3 billion, as exports grew more than imports. Exports jumped 45.5% year-on-year to MYR 191.1 billion, mainly boosted by a 50.4% jump in manufactured goods, which accounted for 88.8% of total exports, particularly E&E products (66.5%). By destination, exports grew sharply to the US (130.4%), Singapore (58.5%), China (19.9%), Taiwan (102.2%), and the EU (20.0%). Meanwhile, imports jumped 41.1% yoy to MYR 163.0 billion, due mainly to a sharp rise in intermediate goods (50.5%) and capital goods (37.4%). In contrast, consumption goods fell 1.6%. Purchases from China surged 61.3%, those from Singapore soared 59.1%, while those from Taiwan jumped 68.9%. In the first eight months of the year, the trade surplus jumped 127.6% yoy to MYR 198.7 billion, with exports rising 31.2% and imports advancing 22.4%.

【上半年A股上市公司繳稅2.5萬億元 稅負平穩】今年以來一些上市公司披露補稅公告引起市場關注，但整體來看上市公司稅負平穩。 從稅務部門瞭解到，根據上市公司公開財務資料，今年上半年，A股上市公司繳納各項稅費2.53萬億元，同比增長4.5%，這一增速明顯低於營業收入（7.6%）和淨利潤（19.5%）增速，也低於上半年全國稅收收入增速（5.3%）。今年上半年A股公司綜合稅負（繳納稅費佔營業收入的比重）較去年同期降低0.2個百分點至6.7%。今年上半年上市公司繳納稅收佔稅務部門徵收的稅收收入比重接近26%，自2023年以來基本穩定在這個水平。 那麼為何今年以來披露補稅的上市公司數量和金額有所增多？這主要是上市公司合規意識尤其是稅法遵從度提升，以及對涉稅事項的披露意願明顯增強。（第一財經）

### 2026-09-18 11:48:43  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Copper Holds Gains on Stronger China Demand — Copper futures held above $6.55 per pound on Friday after advancing for three straight sessions, supported by signs of stronger demand from China. The Yangshan premium, a key indicator of Chinese copper demand, climbed to $121 a ton on Thursday, reaching its highest level since November 2022. Copper was also supported by expectations of sustained long-term consumption from data centers and renewable energy projects, along with supply disruptions at several major mines. Earlier in the week, however, copper prices dropped to multi-week lows following reports that the Trump administration had postponed a decision on potential tariffs on refined copper. Meanwhile, warehouses monitored by the London Metal Exchange recorded fresh deliveries, with inflows reaching their highest level in nearly four weeks. The increase in inventories pushed London copper into contango, pointing to ample availability in the near term.

Rupee Rebounds on Lower Oil, IPO Inflows — The Indian rupee strengthened to around 95.7 per dollar, rebounding from seven-week lows as easing crude oil prices, expectations of foreign portfolio inflows linked to the National Stock Exchange’s $2.3 billion IPO and likely RBI intervention supported the currency. Brent crude fell for a third straight session to around $104.1 a barrel as hopes of alternative routes for Middle Eastern oil to reach markets outweighed supply concerns, while a modest retreat in US bond yields also provided relief. Traders expect sizeable foreign institutional investor demand for the NSE IPO, which closes for subscription on Monday. The rupee held near 96 per dollar on Thursday despite the Federal Reserve’s rate hike, recovering after likely RBI intervention and portfolio inflows linked to global equity index rebalancing. However, strong importer dollar demand and elevated oil prices remain headwinds, while the dollar stays above 100 following the Fed’s hawkish stance.

【渣打：美聯儲主席沃什可能已製造了賣出美元的障礙】渣打銀行表示，美聯儲主席凱文·沃什可能已經移除了買入美元的最大障礙，同時製造了賣出美元的最大障礙。渣打銀行G10外匯研究全球主管Steve Englander在9月17日致客戶的報告中寫道，之前買入美元的最大障礙是貨幣貶值擔憂。他補充說，賣出美元的最大障礙是，名義和實際利差讓美元作為融資貨幣缺乏吸引力，使得美元資產持有者的對沖成本高昂。Englander認為，沃什可能已經消減了美元短線上漲的阻力，因為聯邦公開市場委員會加息25個基點，讓市場對追逐高收益率更放心，而且增加了對沖成本。他表示，目前美元面臨的最大風險是美國經濟增長前景下滑。

### 2026-09-18 11:14:02  #央行 #觀點 #國際

【東方金誠白雪：美聯儲不會開啟連續加息週期】東方金誠研究發展部高階副總監白雪9月18日表示，美聯儲年內再加息一次的機率顯著上升，但這並不意味著美聯儲將開啟連續加息週期。首先，當前美國經濟雖有韌性，但K型分化特徵明顯，居民儲蓄率偏低、消費可持續性下降的問題逐步顯現，AI投資週期也可能面臨邊際走弱。其次，長端美債收益率已攀升至2007年以來高位，金融條件的自發收緊已對利率敏感部門形成實質性壓制，美聯儲仍需謹慎評估緊縮政策的累積效應。再次，本次加息的核心目的是鎖定通脹預期、防範通脹再度固化，屬於階段性政策調整，而非應對經濟過熱和通脹螺旋式抬升的系統性緊縮。而當前美國通脹只是出現階段性反彈，並未形成工資—物價螺旋，基本面也不支援持續加息。9月加息落地後，若12月通脹資料仍偏強，則可能再加息一次。隨後美聯儲將進入觀察期，在4.00%—4.25%的利率水平上觀察通脹向目標回落的進度，再決定下一步是維持、加息還是轉向降息。

### 2026-09-18 11:12:11  #其他

【北環線支線香港段鐵路方案獲授權進行】香港特區政府今日（18日）按《鐵路條例》（第519章）的規定，就運輸及物流局局長批准北環線支線香港段鐵路方案刊登憲報。北環線專案（包括北環線主線和支線）是推動北部都會區發展和助力香港更好融入國家發展的重要運輸基建，配合國家“十五五”規劃綱要提出加快北部都會區建設，促進軌道交通協同發展。北環線支線包括從主線新田站開始一條約六公里長的地下鐵路，當中香港段長約五公里，深圳段則長約一公里。北環線支線將設三個新鐵路站，包括香港段位於新田科技城的洲頭和河套地區兩個新鐵路站，以及深圳段於新皇崗口岸設定的一個新鐵路站。 作為北環線專案的跨境分支，除服務新田科技城及河套地區外，北環線支線亦連線新皇崗口岸，連通香港和深圳的地鐵網路，便利港深兩地人員流動，兼備香港本地通勤和跨境鐵路服務的功能，有助推動粵港澳大灣區基礎設施“硬聯通”。 北環線支線香港段鐵路方案於刊憲期間沒有收到反對意見，足見專案一直獲得社會的廣泛支援。隨著鐵路方案獲授權進行，北環線支線香港段的建造工程會盡快展開，目標是讓北環線支線與主線在2034年或之前同步開通，屆時往來新田和新皇崗口岸的行程預計只需約11分鐘。（大灣區之聲）

### 2026-09-18 11:12:01  #Trading Economics #Markets #China #Currency #Importance 2

Offshore Yuan Strongest Since 2022 — The offshore yuan strengthened to around 6.69 per dollar on Friday, extending gains from the previous session and reaching its strongest level since July 2022, as a firmer daily fixing from the People’s Bank of China continued to support the currency. The central bank set the daily midpoint at 6.7521 per dollar, stronger than Thursday’s 6.7580, marking an upward bias in the fixing for an eighth consecutive session and the longest such run since 2023. The move indicated that the central bank may be increasingly comfortable with a stronger yuan ahead of President Donald Trump’s meeting with Chinese leader Xi Jinping in the US on September 24. A stronger currency could help alleviate some trade tensions between the two economies, as Western officials have argued that an undervalued yuan gives Chinese exporters a competitive edge. Meanwhile, US Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent is also set to meet Chinese Vice Premier He Lifeng this weekend.

BoJ Hikes Rate to 31-Year High — The Bank of Japan lifted its key short-term rate by 25bps to 1.25% in a 7-2 vote at its September meeting, marking the highest level since April 1995 and in line with market expectations. The hike, the first in three months, reflected persistent inflationary pressures, including those stemming from higher oil prices, and marked another step away from decades of ultra-low rates. Policymakers said they would continue to raise the policy rate and adjust monetary accommodation in response to economic activity, prices and financial conditions, while considering the timing and pace of further adjustments. Core CPI inflation has been rising moderately as higher business-to-business prices spill over into consumer prices and firms pass higher wages on to selling prices. Upside risks of inflation exceeding the 2% target remain, with the board adding that it would closely monitor the impact of the Middle East conflict. The dissenting votes came from board members Toichiro Asada and Ayano Sato.

Soybeans Remain Firm Ahead of Trump-Xi Talks — Soybean futures traded above $13.1 per bushel, near their highest level in almost three years supported by stronger Chinese demand for US supplies and expectations of further purchases ahead of high-level US-China talks. China has already passed the halfway mark of its commitment to purchase 25 million metric tons of US soybeans, while recent US export sales have remained firm, confirmed at about 1.7 million metric tons, driven largely by Chinese demand. The buying comes ahead of a planned meeting between US President Donald Trump and Chinese President Xi Jinping next week, raising hopes for progress on agricultural trade and potentially lower tariffs on US farm goods. Meanwhile attention remains on the US harvest, with heavy Midwest rains slowing fieldwork in some areas and potentially providing near-term support to prices. Brazil’s 2026/27 crop is also in focus as planting gets underway, with forecasts pointing to another record harvest, although growth is expected to be modest.

Indonesian Shares Track Back-to-Back Weekly Losses — Indonesia’s IDX Composite added 27 points or 0.4% to 6,488 in Friday morning trade, extending prior gains after Wall Street’s overnight rally as oil prices eased and Treasury yields slipped in the wake of the Fed’s rate hike shock. Domestically, Finance Minister Suahasil Nazara vowed to uphold a credible budget, with economists noting his fiscal expertise and long tenure at the ministry. Strength in energy, tech, and basic materials underpinned the advance, though losses in financials and infrastructure capped momentum. Notable movers included Mitra Adiperkasa (3.4%), Medco Energi (1.6%), Hartadinata Abadi (1.4%), and Vale Indonesia (1.1%). However, the market remains on track for a second consecutive weekly decline, down about 0.8% so far, pressured by growing inflation concerns tied to higher oil prices and looming El Niño risks. Caution also lingered ahead of Bank Indonesia’s policy meeting next week, with renewed rupiah weakness and signs of foreign outflows weighing on sentiment.

New Zealand Dollar Falls Back — The New Zealand dollar slipped to around $0.571 on Friday, moving back near a ten-week low after rising 0.3% in the previous session, as a firmer US dollar continued to weigh on the currency. The greenback clung to a seven-week high after the Federal Reserve hiked rates and signaled further tightening ahead. This outweighed support from better-than-expected domestic data. New Zealand’s GDP growth in the second quarter beat estimates, suggesting some resilience in economic activity despite the severe energy shock from US-Iran war. In addition, the country’s trade deficit for August was smaller than forecast, with export growth outpacing the increase in imports. Meanwhile, markets are pricing in a 60% chance that the Reserve Bank of New Zealand will raise its cash rate again to 3.0% at its October meeting. The kiwi was down around 1.6% so far this week, marking its fourth consecutive weekly decline.

### 2026-09-18 10:40:49  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

US Natgas Prices Drop Over 1% — US natural gas prices fell more than 1% to $2.85/MMBtu on Friday, erasing the previous session’s gains as weather forecasts turned cooler. Above-average temperatures are now expected to cover a smaller area of the South and Southeast from September 22 to October 1, potentially curbing gas demand from power generators for air conditioning. Prices had risen on Thursday after the EIA reported a below-average storage build for the week ended September 11, narrowing the inventory surplus over the five-year average to 118 Bcf from 148 Bcf a week earlier, as late-season heat boosted power-sector demand. Strong production also continued to weigh on the market, with Lower 48 production averaging 113.1 bcfd in September, above August’s record monthly average of 112.2 bcfd. 【住房城鄉建設部：現房銷售是大勢所趨】國新辦舉行“開局起步‘十五五’”系列主題新聞釋出會。住房城鄉建設部有關負責人介紹，當前，房地產市場出現了“兩個轉變”。一個是房地產市場供求關係發生重大變化；一個是房地產進入存量時代，二手房交易佔比從2020年的27%提高到2025年的46%，今年前8個月已經達到52%，超過50%標誌著進入存量時代。“十五五”時期，住房城鄉建設部將積極適應房地產的新形勢新變化，統籌防風險和促轉型，統籌惠民生和穩增長，加快構建房地產發展新模式，健全多主體供給、多渠道保障、租購併舉的住房制度，推動房地產高質量發展。在商品房銷售上，推進現房銷售制，解決專案銷售“一手交錢，一手交房”的問題，實現“所見即所得”，從根本上防範交付風險。現房銷售是大勢所趨，從國家統計局資料來看，近年來現房的銷售面積保持正增長，佔新房銷售面積的比重逐年提高。新專案優先選擇現房銷售，統籌用好針對專案的土地、金融、稅收等支援政策；實行預售的，完善預售資金監管，切實維護購房人合法權益。

【美銀證券：贛鋒鋰業鋰價回撥反映多重憂慮 公司指市場需求預期已過於悲觀】美銀證券釋出研報稱，贛鋒鋰業(01772)在電話會議上討論近期鋰價弱勢，似乎反映多重市場憂慮，包括主要行業參與者削減訂單的傳聞、電池出貨強勁但下游儲能裝機及電動車銷售未跟上所引發的供需錯配及庫存累積憂慮、上海有色網(SMM)資料修訂所引發的隱性庫存猜測，以及高倉單未必代表庫存充裕等問題。該行維持"買入"評級，H股目標價70港元。該行指，贛鋒不同意悲觀論調，不認為電池持有大量庫存，公司庫存偏低、行業偏緊，現貨供需非常緊張，今年處於短缺且去庫存持續，並非需求崩潰的市場。公司假設明年供應增長20%至25%、需求增長約25%，認為市場需求預期已過於悲觀;公平鋰價約為15萬元人民幣，但10萬至12萬元人民幣是關鍵支撐，成本上升快於預期，在12萬元人民幣水平已可見供應減少。

【Ministry: housing and urban construction faces new dynamics in 15th Five-Year Plan period】At a State Council Information Office briefing today, the Ministry of Housing and Urban-Rural Development said the housing and urban-rural development sector will face new dynamics during the 15th Five-Year Plan period. The ministry identified three main segments—urban-rural construction, housing and real estate, and the construction industry—each at a critical transformation point. For urban-rural construction it described two shifts: urbanization is moving from rapid expansion to stable growth, and city development is shifting from large-scale incremental expansion to stock-focused quality and efficiency improvements. For housing and real estate it cited two transitions: a significant change in market supply-demand dynamics and a move into a stock era. For the construction industry it outlined a threefold transformation toward intelligence, greening and integration.

【中國三峽建工（集團）有限公司原一級諮詢陳文夫被"雙開"】中央紀委國家監委網站9月18日通報，據中央紀委國家監委駐中國三峽集團紀檢監察組、湖北省紀委監委訊息：日前，經中央紀委國家監委批准，中央紀委國家監委駐中國三峽集團紀檢監察組、湖北省宜昌市監委對中國三峽建工（集團）有限公司原一級諮詢陳文夫嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。經查，陳文夫作為黨員領導幹部，喪失理想信念，背棄初心使命，對黨不忠誠不老實，在組織談話時不如實說清問題，妄圖透過社會騙子規避組織調查，與他人串供，處心積慮對抗組織審查；無視中央八項規定精神，收受可能影響公正執行公務的禮品、禮金，接受可能影響公正執行公務的宴請和娛樂活動安排；違反組織原則，在職工錄用中為他人謀取利益；廉潔底線失守，向管理服務物件借用錢款，參與民間借貸獲取大額回報，將應由特定關係人個人支付的費用安排管理服務物件支付；追求低階趣味；貪慾膨脹，將權力異化為謀取私利的工具，利用職務便利為他人在工程專案承攬、結算等方面謀取利益，並非法收受鉅額財物。陳文夫嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律、廉潔紀律、生活紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經中國三峽集團黨組研究，決定給予陳文夫開除黨籍處分；經中央紀委國家監委駐中國三峽集團紀檢監察組研究，決定給予陳文夫開除公職處分；追繳其違紀違法所得。湖北省宜昌市監委將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。

### 2026-09-18 10:00:24  #宏觀

【市場監管總局釋出首個鈣鈦礦光伏領域國家標準】市場監管總局釋出首個鈣鈦礦光伏領域國家標準《光伏器件 第12部分：鈣鈦礦光伏電池及元件的電流-電壓（I-V）特性測量方法》，將於2027年正式實施。在“雙碳”目標推動下，鈣鈦礦光伏技術發展迅速，正從實驗室走向吉瓦級大規模量產，電池及元件的測試認證需求不斷增長。但鈣鈦礦器件在光照和電壓變化下效能容易波動，測試結果不夠穩定，面積定義也尚未統一，過去常用於晶矽和薄膜電池的測試方法難以直接套用。簡單來說，該標準要解決的是鈣鈦礦電池和元件到底該怎麼測才能測得準、比得公平。鈣鈦礦器件在光照下效能會變化，不能像傳統晶矽那樣快速一測即可。該標準系統規定了測試裝置、測試前的穩定化處理要求，以及如何測量電流-電壓特性、如何計算面積和發電效率等。針對實驗室研發和規模化生產的不同需要，該標準給出快速測量法，並補充最大功率點追蹤法、十點準穩態擬合法（仲裁法）、漸進法等更貼近真實工作狀態的準穩態測試方法。同時，該標準統一總面積與光闌面積定義及效率計算，讓不同機構測出的結果可以放在同一把標尺上比較，最終形成一套可驗證、可復現的標準化測試流程。該標準構建了科學、可靠、可推廣的鈣鈦礦光伏I-V特性測量方法體系，填補了我國在新興光伏技術標準化測試領域的空白。它將成為檢測認證、產品研發、產線質控和貿易交付的“統一標尺”，為後續標準體系完善、國際標準制定及鈣鈦礦光伏產業化推廣應用提供關鍵技術支撐。

### 2026-09-18 09:54:05  #其他

【加強涉企侵權資訊管理 中央網信辦要求平臺主動清理五大類】中央網信辦釋出《關於加強涉企侵權資訊管理 持續推動營商網路環境最佳化的通知》，通知明確處置範圍，內容如下：處置涉企侵權資訊，事關企業、企業家切身利益，事關人民群眾切身權益。經過一段時間實踐，各方對處置涉企侵權資訊已形成共識。網站平臺對以下涉企侵權資訊，應當積極主動清理處置。1.個人資訊類。包括洩露企業家身份證、護照、戶籍檔案、家庭住址、電話號碼等個人身份及聯絡資訊，以及生物識別、醫療健康、金融賬戶、行蹤軌跡等敏感個人資訊。2.侮辱貶損類。包括侮辱謾罵企業、企業家，惡搞醜化企業標識、產品和企業家肖像，將企業、企業家與色情低俗話題惡意關聯，使用汙名化詞彙詆譭企業、企業家或特定品牌使用者。3.假冒仿冒類。包括假冒仿冒新聞單位制作釋出的涉企監督資訊，假借企業或企業家名義釋出的資訊，在公眾賬號名稱、頭像、簡介中，擅自使用與企業相同或相似的名稱標識或企業家姓名肖像。4.虛假誤導類。包括斷章取義、歪曲解讀企業家過往言論，呈現與事實不符的搜尋關鍵詞聯想、搜尋落地頁結果，已經權威闢謠、澄清的不實資訊，與企業、企業家在超大平臺上的官方賬號釋出的公開宣告不符資訊。5.其他類。包括涉企拉踩引戰資訊，集納翻炒的企業舊聞舊事、負面資訊，已被本平臺處置過的涉企侵權資訊，機器人賬號、網路水軍賬號釋出的涉企評論資訊，明顯違背公序良俗抹黑詆譭企業、企業家資訊。網站平臺應當根據本通知要求，健全資訊內容管理制度，完善最佳化平臺社群規則，將涉企資訊納入日常內容稽核處置範圍，強化賬號、MCN機構管理，切實增強工作主動性、針對性和實效性。網信部門要落實屬地管理責任，指導所在地網站平臺完善制度，加強涉企資訊內容管理，及時清理涉企侵權資訊。對重視程度不足、落實力度不夠特別是網民反映問題集中的網站平臺，依法依規從嚴處理。

Australia 10Y Yield Retreats from 2011 Highs — Australia’s 10-year government bond yield fell below 5.3%, pulling back from mid-2011 highs and tracking lower global yields as easing concerns over Middle East supply disruptions reduced inflation pressures. Gasoline Prices Ease — US gasoline futures fell to around $3.46 per gallon, slightly easing from an eight-week high, as expectations that Saudi Arabia could restore some disrupted exports soothed immediate supply concerns. The kingdom was reportedly seeking to restore about half the capacity of its East-West oil pipeline within days after drone attacks halted the key link to the Red Sea, while offering additional crude cargoes to Asian refiners via ship-to-ship transfers off Oman’s Sohar port. Nevertheless, risks remained as Saudi Arabia and Iran-backed Houthi forces exchanged fresh strikes. Meanwhile, EIA data showed US gasoline inventories unexpectedly rose for a second consecutive week, increasing by 794,000 barrels in the week ended September 11, while refinery utilization remained elevated at 96.8%, despite easing from 97.8% a week earlier. However, approaching seasonal refinery maintenance could constrain production and remains a threat to refined-product supplies.

US Heating Oil Prices Ease — US heating oil prices eased to around $5.05 per gallon, retreating from a record high of $5.26 reached earlier this week, as rising inventories and expectations of alternative routes for Middle Eastern barrels offered some respite from tight distillate supplies. EIA data showed distillate inventories, including diesel and heating oil, rose by 1.6 million barrels in the week ended September 11 but remained 13% below their five-year average. Meanwhile, Saudi Arabia was reportedly seeking to restore about half the capacity of its East-West oil pipeline within days after drone attacks halted the key link to the Red Sea, while offering additional crude cargoes to Asian refiners via ship-to-ship transfers off Oman’s Sohar port. Still, supply risks persisted as Saudi Arabia and Iran-backed Houthi forces exchanged fresh strikes. With winter approaching, distillate demand could strengthen, while refinery maintenance could constrain production and put further pressure on already-tight supplies.

【NDRC, NEA and national mine safety regulator order measures to stabilize coal production and supply】China's NDRC, NEA and the National Mine Safety Administration issued a joint notice directing coal-producing provinces and coal firms to, while ensuring safety, accelerate measures to stabilize production and secure supply. The notice mandates stronger monitoring and dispatch, optimized production planning, expedited mine resumptions and full-capacity restarts, and faster acceptance of jointly trial-run mines to support a steady rebound in coal output. It also requires tighter signing and performance of medium- and long-term thermal-coal contracts, use of imports to supplement domestic supply, and advancement of follow-up coal capacity construction. Authorities said they will enhance coordination, guide implementation, adjust policies and, where appropriate, timely release coal-capacity reserves to stabilize production and market functioning.

【Liquidity indicators: market rates continue to rebound】Early on Sept. 18 DR007 rebounded to 1.4550%, trading around the PBOC 7‑day policy rate near 1.4%. The PBOC injected 162 billion yuan via 7‑day reverse repos and, for the first time in nearly a year, conducted a 14‑day reverse repo of 100 billion yuan. DR007 is a short‑term funding benchmark; its spread versus the PBOC 7‑day repo is used to gauge funding tightness—DR007 above the policy rate signals tighter market funding, below signals looser conditions.

【加拿大對進口罐裝蔬菜產品作出保障措施終裁】近日，WTO保障措施委員會發布加拿大代表團向其提交的保障措施通報。加拿大國際貿易法庭（CITT）於2026年9月9日對進口罐頭蔬菜產品（Canned Vegetable Goods）作出保障措施肯定性終裁，認定進口增加是造成國內產業嚴重損害的主要原因，建議以關稅配額形式實施保障措施。具體如下：第1年配額為13,000,000公斤，超配額附加稅為50%；第2年配額為13,260,000公斤，超配額附加稅為45%；第3年配額為13,525,200公斤，超配額附加稅為40%。措施建議實施3年，至2030年1月5日。

### 2026-09-18 09:18:13  #其他

【中央網信辦：加強涉企侵權資訊管理 持續推動營商網路環境最佳化】中央網信辦釋出《關於加強涉企侵權資訊管理 持續推動營商網路環境最佳化的通知》，通知指出，處置涉企侵權資訊，事關企業、企業家切身利益，事關人民群眾切身權益。經過一段時間實踐，各方對處置涉企侵權資訊已形成共識。網站平臺對以下涉企侵權資訊，應當積極主動清理處置。1.個人資訊類。包括洩露企業家身份證、護照、戶籍檔案、家庭住址、電話號碼等個人身份及聯絡資訊，以及生物識別、醫療健康、金融賬戶、行蹤軌跡等敏感個人資訊。2.侮辱貶損類。包括侮辱謾罵企業、企業家，惡搞醜化企業標識、產品和企業家肖像，將企業、企業家與色情低俗話題惡意關聯，使用汙名化詞彙詆譭企業、企業家或特定品牌使用者。3.假冒仿冒類。包括假冒仿冒新聞單位制作釋出的涉企監督資訊，假借企業或企業家名義釋出的資訊，在公眾賬號名稱、頭像、簡介中，擅自使用與企業相同或相似的名稱標識或企業家姓名肖像。4.虛假誤導類。包括斷章取義、歪曲解讀企業家過往言論，呈現與事實不符的搜尋關鍵詞聯想、搜尋落地頁結果，已經權威闢謠、澄清的不實資訊，與企業、企業家在超大平臺上的官方賬號釋出的公開宣告不符資訊。5.其他類。包括涉企拉踩引戰資訊，集納翻炒的企業舊聞舊事、負面資訊，已被本平臺處置過的涉企侵權資訊，機器人賬號、網路水軍賬號釋出的涉企評論資訊，明顯違背公序良俗抹黑詆譭企業、企業家資訊。

### 2026-09-18 09:15:47  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Japan 10-Year Yield Falls Ahead of BOJ Verdict — Japan’s 10-year government bond yield slipped to around 2.96% on Friday, extending its decline into a third session as markets positioned for the Bank of Japan’s latest policy decision, with markets widely expecting an interest-rate hike. Investors will also watch for signals on further tightening as policymakers navigate rising inflation and wage growth, along with pressure from US Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent to pursue more aggressive rate increases. Meanwhile, Japan’s core inflation eased to 1.7% in August from 1.8% in July, marking its first slowdown in four months. However, expectations that price pressures could strengthen in the coming months continued to support a hawkish BOJ outlook, while disruptions in the Middle East added to inflation risks. Japanese bond yields also followed declines in global yields as falling oil prices helped ease concerns over inflation.

Yen Slips Ahead of BOJ Decision — The Japanese yen weakened past 156 per dollar on Friday, hovering near two-week lows as investors awaited the latest Bank of Japan policy decision, with markets widely expecting an interest-rate hike. Investors will also seek guidance on the potential for further tightening as policymakers contend with rising inflation and wages, alongside pressure from US Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent for a more aggressive pace of rate increases. Meanwhile, Japan’s core inflation eased to 1.7% in August from 1.8% in July, marking its first slowdown in four months. However, expectations that price pressures could strengthen in the coming months continued to support a hawkish BOJ outlook, with disruptions stemming from the Middle East adding to inflation risks. Earlier this month, the yen climbed to seven-month highs on expectations of more aggressive BOJ tightening, recent joint interventions by Tokyo and Washington, and prospects of greater capital repatriation by Japanese investors.

【國泰海通：地方新增專項債發行節奏偏慢 預計四季度專項債用途更靈活】國泰海通證券釋出的研報稱，2026年地方政府新增專項債發行節奏偏慢，或與專案儲備不足有關，預計四季度專項債用途或更加靈活，支援化債，供給放量但久期壓力減弱。截至9月11日，全年新增專項債發行金額3.03萬億，佔全年新增專項債限額的68.9%；按照發行計劃推算，9月末進度將達到76.4%。從過去幾年的發行節奏看，9月末通常完成全年限額約九成，10月末專項債基本發完。年內地方債剩餘發行金額預計在2.28萬億元，年內剩餘地方債的發行期限在15.3年附近，較前期有較為明顯下降。進入四季度，新增專項債在當年形成實物工作量的難度加大，部分專案儲備不足的省份可能會增加特殊新增專項債的發行，用途更為靈活。

### 2026-09-18 09:06:35  #MKT News #Important #Market Regions #Japan & APAC #USDJPY #Impact bullish

【BOJ decision preview: 25bp hike expected; yen vulnerable if guidance isn’t hawkish】Most firms expect the BOJ to raise its policy rate by 25bp to 1.25% today; markets are focussing on forward guidance — a less hawkish signal would likely trigger yen weakness and pressure JGBs. Reuters survey: economists expect the policy rate to reach 1.50% by end-March next year and 1.75% by Q2 2027, with most seeing at least that terminal rate. Nikko expects 25bp to 1.25% and 1.75% by June 2027, with upside to 2.25% if oil rises and other central banks tighten. BNP sees 25bp now, then further hikes in Dec and next March to 1.75%. OCBC says the hike is largely priced; the key is the post-September path and whether the BOJ signals faster normalization while inflation remains elevated. Goldman says today’s move is certain and the next hike could come as early as December; high energy, strong AI-driven demand, a weaker yen and loose fiscal policy could push inflation and yields higher. TD Securities projects a path to 2.25% via hikes in Dec and several 2027 moves and warns that omission of Oct/Dec hikes from guidance could send USD/JPY to 157–160. Market pricing already implies ~90bp of tightening over the next 12 months; if Governor Ueda signals caution, that could leave the yen exposed. Overall market takeaway: 25bp hike probable; guidance tone will be the primary driver for near-term yen moves, JGB yields and expectations for further BOJ tightening.

### 2026-09-18 08:59:07  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Currency #Importance 1

Australian Dollar Attempts Rebound — The Australian dollar rose above $0.71, attempting to recover from a recent four-week low as hawkish comments from the Reserve Bank’s top official reinforced expectations for further policy tightening. Governor Michele Bullock said upside inflation risks were materializing and warned that higher costs could make inflation more persistent, pointing to rising energy prices and continued capacity pressures in the domestic economy. Her remarks follow statements over the past month by senior RBA officials that inflation remains elevated and needs to ease further. Swaps markets now priced at least two more rate hikes by the February meeting, with a roughly 87% chance the first occurs on September 29. That marks a notable shift from the start of September, when traders were pricing just one further hike, likely by year-end. The Aussie also benefited from a softer US dollar, alongside lower Treasury yields and easing oil prices, while a rebound in global equities improved risk sentiment.

Silver Holds Gains on Lower Oil Prices — Silver held above $65 an ounce on Friday after climbing more than 3% in the previous session, supported by falling oil prices that eased inflation concerns and helped push bond yields lower. Oil prices declined for a third consecutive session as Saudi Arabia worked to restore flows through its East-West pipeline, while President Donald Trump is set to meet with Gulf leaders next week. Treasury yields also pulled back from multi-year highs, with the US 10-year yield falling to around 4.93% after briefly exceeding 5% earlier this week. Meanwhile, investors continued to assess the outlook for Federal Reserve monetary policy following its first rate hike in three years. The US central bank signaled that additional tightening could be needed to contain inflationary pressures, with markets now pricing in roughly a 53% probability of a move in October.

【CITIC Securities: gas-turbine industry in new upcycle】CITIC Securities says the gas-turbine industry is in a new upcycle. Demand for small- and mid-sized turbines is rising faster owing to flexible deployment and shorter construction cycles. Overseas OEM capacity expansion has long lead times, keeping supply tight; bottlenecks in hot-end components and large rotating parts are transmitting upstream. Overseas supply gaps and constrained deliveries of core engines for aviation fuel reform are likely to shift demand to domestic complete-machine and component makers, boosting demand for upstream materials such as high-temperature alloys.

### 2026-09-18 08:09:53  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Food Inflation #Importance 1

Japan Food Prices Rise the Least in 25 Months — Japan’s food prices rose 3.1% year-on-year in August 2026, slowing from 3.5% in the previous month and marking the slowest increase since July 2024. Price pressures moderated across fresh vegetables (5.5% vs 6.5% in July), fresh fruit (0.1% vs 2.4%), dairy products and eggs (1.3% vs 3.5%), cakes and candies (5.0% vs 6.1%), meats (3.3% vs 3.9%), alcoholic beverages (0.4% vs 0.6%), and prepared foods (3.3% vs 3.5%). Meanwhile, cereal prices fell for a fourth straight month (-3.4% vs -2.2%). At the same time, rice prices dropped 15.7% year-on-year, extending their decline for a fourth consecutive month and marking the steepest fall since April 2005. In contrast, prices accelerated for fish and seafood (9.9% vs 9.0%), oils, fats and seasonings (4.0% vs 3.4%), beverages (6.0% vs 5.5%), and meals eaten out (2.1% vs 1.7%).

【Huatai Securities: Big three airlines' August load factors strong; sector rebound awaits oil-price decline】Huatai Securities reports that in August peak travel the big three carriers (Air China, China Eastern, China Southern) plus Spring Airlines and Juneyao saw ASK +5.2% YoY and RPK +7.6% YoY; load factor rose 2ppt to a record 88.8% and ticket pricing showed some improvement. However, July–August domestic fares including fuel surcharges rose only 1.0% YoY—extreme weather and demand/capacity mismatches limited pricing and weakened oil-price pass-through. Huatai says a sector rebound will await lower oil prices; monitor high-frequency Mid-Autumn and National Day travel data. It expects cautious capacity deployment in Q4, which could support YoY revenue performance, and remains constructive on a mid-to-long-term recovery in the transport sector.

### 2026-09-18 07:57:24  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 2

US Futures Steady After Upbeat Session — US stock futures were little changed on Friday after the major averages rallied in the previous session, as falling oil prices and bond yields improved risk appetite. In regular trading on Thursday, the Dow rose 0.61%, the S&P 500 gained 1.14%, and the Nasdaq Composite advanced 1.69%. Chipmakers led the gains, extending their recovery after concerns over AI safety earlier in the week sparked doubts about AI-related capital spending. Strong performances came from Micron (5.5%), Nvidia (2.5%), Intel (7.7%), AMD (6.4%), and SanDisk (6.2%). Stocks benefited from lower oil prices that eased inflation worries and helped Treasuries rally across the curve, with the US 10-year yield falling to around 4.93% after topping 5% earlier this week. Wall Street also rallied in relief after the Federal Reserve delivered a widely expected rate hike, though policymakers signaled further tightening to curb inflation.

### 2026-09-18 07:37:18  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Core Inflation Rate #Importance 2

Japan Core Inflation Falls Below Forecasts — Japan’s core consumer price index, which excludes fresh food but includes energy, increased 1.7% year-on-year in August 2026, after a 1.8% rise in July. The latest reading slowed from the highest rate in six months, touched a month earlier, and was below the market forecast of 1.8%. Still, the latest reading remained below the Bank of Japan’s 2% target for an eighth straight month, as government fuel subsidies helped offset price pressures from higher oil costs linked to the Middle East conflict.

【鎢價暴漲後深度回撥 概念股上半年業績集體高增】鎢價經歷年初衝高、二季度大幅回撥後，三季度轉入高位窄幅震盪。雖然當前價格較3月曆史高點顯著回撥，但相比兩年前漲幅仍接近2倍。受國內開採配額管控，鎢供給增長空間有限，在戰略金屬屬性加持下鎢價格中樞仍有支撐。A股市場鎢礦概念股共計6只，隨著鎢價持續高位執行，產業鏈企業盈利能力大幅釋放，上半年板塊業績集體迎來爆發。從淨利潤規模看，洛陽鉬業、廈門鎢業、中鎢高新排名前三，依次為161.52億元、22.01億元、20.76億元；章源鎢業、翔鷺鎢業、中稀有色歸母淨利潤也超過4億元。從淨利潤變動看，翔鷺鎢業、章源鎢業、中稀有色、中鎢高新、廈門鎢業上半年歸母淨利潤均同比翻倍增長。翔鷺鎢業最高，增幅高達2522.40%；章源鎢業、中稀有色隨後，增幅均在400%以上。

【國際金價坐上“過山車” 多因素仍支撐中長期黃金配置】全球黃金市場在美聯儲議息結果公佈前後經歷了戲劇性的一幕。在當地時間9月16日美聯儲利率決議公佈前，國際金價一度衝高至4360美元/盎司上方，日內漲幅逾1%；在加息“靴子”落地後，金價迅速跳水，最終收跌0.7%至4263.13美元/盎司，為近四周低位。黃金不生息的特性，在利率上行環境中再次暴露其脆弱的一面。A股貴金屬板塊同樣反應劇烈。北京時間9月17日，該板塊領跌，收跌超過5%。然而當日亞歐交易時段，倫敦現貨黃金價格卻震盪攀升，重新站上4300美元/盎司。截至記者發稿時，倫敦金報4326.9美元/盎司，漲1.5%。市場原本預期這會是一次“鴿派”加息，實際落地時卻亮出“鷹爪”——美聯儲不僅時隔三年多首次加息，還釋放出年內或進一步收緊貨幣政策的訊號。受訪人士認為，金價仍以區間震盪為主，短期走強可視為利空出盡，中期美國財政壓力和美元信用擔憂等仍支撐黃金配置。（上海證券報）

Brent Slides for Third Session — Brent crude fell to around $104 per barrel on Friday, extending its decline for a third consecutive session as concerns over supply disruptions in the Middle East eased and hopes grew for renewed diplomatic efforts to end the conflict and restore energy flows. Saudi Arabia is targeting the recovery of around half the capacity of its East-West pipeline within days, with a return to full operations expected within six weeks. In the meantime, the kingdom is rerouting some crude exports through the Strait of Hormuz. Shuttle vessels transport crude through Hormuz before loading tankers waiting outside the strait, limiting their exposure to potential Iranian attacks. Reports also suggested that China had urged Iran to help curb Houthi militants after an appeal from Riyadh, as the rebel group intensified attacks on Saudi energy infrastructure. Meanwhile, President Donald Trump said he was weighing whether to resume attacks on Iran ahead of a meeting with Gulf leaders in New York next week.

Costa Rica Unemployment Rate Falls to 7.0% in Q2 — The unemployment rate in Costa Rica fell to 7.0% in the second quarter of 2026 from 7.4% in the same period a year earlier, but little changed from 7.1% in the previous quarter. Nationwide, the number of unemployed people was estimated at 164,000, including 98,000 men and 66,000 women. The unemployment rate was 6.8% for men and 7.2% for women. The labor force stood at 2.35 million, comprising 1.43 million men and 921,000 women, while the net labor force participation rate was 54.6%, at 66.3% for men and 42.9% for women. Employment rose to 2.18 million, with the employment rate at 50.8%, compared with 61.7% for men and 39.8% for women. Informal employment totaled 785,000, including 438,000 self-employed workers and 348,000 wage earners or family helpers.

New Zealand Food Inflation Stays at 19-Month Low — Annual food inflation in New Zealand stood at 1.9% in August 2026, unchanged from the previous month, which was the softest increase since December 2024. Prices for meat, poultry, and fish increased by 3.4% (vs 3.2% in July), after marking the slowest rate since at least June 2025 in July, and remained the largest contributor to the annual increase in food prices. Grocery food prices also accelerated (1.7% vs 1.6% in July), while fruit and vegetable prices fell at a faster rate, declining 1.6%, following a 1.0% drop in July.

### 2026-09-18 06:54:22  #公司

【中小股東提案門檻要“降”，行權通道更要“暢”】新《公司法》將股東臨時提案持股門檻降至1%，至今已一年有餘，然而門檻下降並未明顯啟用中小股東的提案熱情。中小股東提案少，並非投資者沒有訴求。近兩年多場上市公司股東會上，關聯交易、股權激勵、修訂規則等方案遭遇中小股東高票反對，部分議案在大股東迴避表決後直接被否決。這說明，中小投資者對公司經營、利益分配有著清晰判斷，參與公司治理的意願持續高漲。可現實是，很多中小股東的合理訴求難以轉化為正式提案，制度供給與實際行權之間仍存在落差。行權成本高是第一道現實門檻。股東行使臨時提案權，需單獨或合計持有1%股份。對於分散股東而言，想要聯合湊齊這一持股比例，會面臨股東資訊隔離、聯絡渠道匱乏等難題，且自發聯合的時間成本、溝通成本高昂。即便湊夠持股條件，相關提案還需要具備清晰議題、可付諸表決的決議事項。普通投資者大多不熟悉相關規則，又缺少專業中介力量支援，不少提案因為格式、文字等形式瑕疵，直接被董事會擋在股東會之外。（證券時報）

Brent Extends Drop as Supply Risks Ease — Brent crude oil settled near $104 a barrel on Thursday, extending a 2.7% drop from the previous session, as Saudi Arabia shifts some crude exports through the Strait of Hormuz to offset the closure of a key pipeline. Saudi Arabia is reportedly making additional crude cargoes available to Asian refiners through ship-to-ship transfers near Oman’s Sohar port. Shuttle vessels transport crude through Hormuz before loading tankers waiting outside the strait, reducing their exposure to Iranian attacks. Saudi Arabia also said it could restore around half of the damaged East-West pipeline’s capacity within days and full operations within six weeks. Saudi crude loadings at Mideast Gulf ports have risen this month, while ship-to-ship transfers in the Gulf of Oman increased to 2.7 million bpd from 1.5 million bpd in August. Meanwhile, Russia’s seaborne oil product exports jumped 16.4% in August from July as refineries returned from maintenance.

South Korea Producer Inflation Rises in August — South Korea’s producer prices rose 7.9% year over year in August 2026, accelerating from a 7.7% increase in July. Manufacturing producer prices climbed 12.2%, led by a 54.3% surge in coal and petroleum products. Prices for computers, electronic and optical equipment rose 24.5%, while chemical products increased 16.9% and basic metal products advanced 16.0%. Meanwhile, prices of agricultural, forestry, and marine products rose 1.1%, while service prices increased 4.4%. Utility prices rose 2.9%. In contrast, prices of mining products edged down 0.1%. On a monthly basis, the Producer Price Index increased 0.2%, following a 0.4% rise in July.

【ANTHROPIC releases metric system to track pace of frontier lab AI development】ANTHROPIC published a public metric framework to measure the pace of frontier lab AI development, aiming to give outsiders clearer data on model R&D progress. The framework targets three areas: AI participation in next‑generation AI R&D, supervisory control over AI agent behavior, and resource投入 into developing stronger models. It includes an AI R&D Automation Index that measures Claude’s share of company R&D. As of Aug 2026, Claude had not achieved fully autonomous R&D in any measured domain but accounted for about 26% of ANTHROPIC’s AI R&D; over 90% of R&D work reached ‘AI collaboration’ or higher.

### 2026-09-18 04:52:18  #Trading Economics #Markets #Brazil #Stock Market #Importance 1

Ibovespa Edges Higher After Selic Cut — The Ibovespa edged 0.2% higher to close at 185,992 on Thursday after a volatile session following the BCB’s decision to cut the Selic rate by 25 bps to 13.75%. The Copom did not signal its next steps but left the door open for another cut at the next meeting. The message was one of continuity, with monetary policy remaining cautious, data-dependent, and focused on composure. Still, the index fell as much as 1.2% during the session amid concerns over government spending and electoral effects after the federal government announced a 15% increase in welfare benefits. Polls showing challenger Flávio Bolsonaro numerically ahead in second-round scenarios continued to support the index. A halt in the oil rally and the consequent softening of global yields also helped support the market. Major banks were mixed, with Banco do Brasil rising 0.3% and Santander advancing 2.5%, while Itaú inched lower and Bradesco fell 0.3%. Vale rose 2.1%, helped by higher iron ore prices.

### 2026-09-18 04:29:46  #Trading Economics #Markets #Canada #Stock Market #Importance 1

TSX Closes Higher on Oil Pullback — Argentina’s unemployment rate rose to 7.9% in the second quarter of 2026, up from 7.8% in the previous quarter, although the change was not statistically significant. The employment rate edged up to 45.0% from 44.8%, while labor force participation increased to 48.9% from 48.6%. By region, unemployment was highest in the Pampas region at 9.7%, followed by Greater Buenos Aires at 8.2%. The jobless rate stood at 8.4% for women and 7.5% for men. Unemployment among women aged 14 to 29 reached 17.1%, while the rate for men in the same age group stood at 14.1%. By education level, the largest share of unemployment was among those with completed secondary education at 2.8% of the labor force. Regarding job-search duration, 2.4% of the labor force had been looking for work for more than a year. Meanwhile, unemployment in urban areas outside Greater Buenos Aires rose significantly, from 6.8% to 7.6%.

### 2026-09-18 04:22:42  #MKT News

【NBC: Pentagon weighing withdrawal of about 25,000 U.S. troops from Europe】NBC, citing seven current and former U.S. and Western officials, reports the Pentagon is assessing a significant drawdown of U.S. forces in Europe that could withdraw roughly 25,000 troops—about one-third of the roughly 80,000 currently stationed there—and could extend to as many as 40,000. The Pentagon launched a six-month review in June; an assessment is expected to be submitted to the defense secretary soon. Options under consideration include cuts to air, naval and ground personnel or relocation of some units. Officials say the review aims to align deployments with the "America First" strategy. Some European governments and U.S. lawmakers warn a large-scale withdrawal could weaken NATO deterrence; supporters argue for rebalancing overseas forces and shifting greater defense burden to European allies. No decision has been made; any withdrawal would require presidential approval.

### 2026-09-18 04:02:23  #Trading Economics #Economy #Argentina #GDP Annual Growth Rate #Importance 1

Argentina GDP Growth Slows in Q2 — Argentina’s economy grew 2.0% year-on-year in the second quarter of 2026, slowing from a 2.3% increase in the previous quarter, but beating forecasts of a 1.6% expansion. The strongest sector gains came from fishing (44.7%), mining and quarrying (16.4%), and agriculture, livestock, hunting and forestry (6.9%). Other notable expansions were recorded in electricity, gas and water (5.0%), financial intermediation (4.8%), and hotels and restaurants (1.6%). In contrast, manufacturing contracted 2.1%, while public administration and defense declined 1.4%, marking the weakest performances. On the demand side, exports rose 13.7% year-on-year, leading growth, while private consumption increased 0.4% and public consumption fell 4.1%. Gross fixed capital formation contracted 11.1%, while imports of goods and services fell 8.6%. On a quarter-on-quarter basis, the economy contracted 0.6% in seasonally adjusted terms.

【摩根大通關於伊朗戰爭以何種方式結束：“我們根本不知道”】伊朗戰爭爆發六個多月後，摩根大通的石油分析師公開表達了許多交易員一段時間以來私下所說的看法：這場戰爭將以何種方式結束，正變得越來越難以預測。包括Natasha Kaneva在內的分析師在一份報告中表示，該行此前認為美國政府不願突破的幾條經濟紅線，包括油價升至每桶100美元以上、汽油價格逼近每加侖5美元以及美國國債收益率飆升，如今都已經出現，這使得退出戰略變得更加不明朗。隨著能源價格飆升，而由此產生的連鎖影響可能破壞全球經濟增長並加劇通脹，石油交易員和分析師越來越不確定伊朗戰爭還會持續多久。包括沙烏地阿拉伯至關重要的東西向輸油管道在內的關鍵能源基礎設施近期遭到襲擊，加劇了市場對供應進一步趨緊的擔憂。分析師們在週四被市場參與者廣泛援引的一份報告中表示：“我們認為，市場目前高度緊張。” 他們表示，9月份石油的合理價值估計約為每桶90美元，儘管目前價格接近106美元，並補充說，這意味著，在目前已經有每日1000萬桶供應受到干擾的基礎上，市場正在計入每日額外損失400萬桶供應的風險。

【US approves PEZESHKIAN and Iran foreign minister to attend UN General Assembly in New York】The U.S. State Department told AP it has approved travel for Iranian President PEZESHKIAN and Foreign Minister Araghchi to attend next week’s UN General Assembly high-level meetings in New York. The department said Iran’s core delegation and necessary staff will be allowed to participate but will face travel restrictions and a U.S. policy ban on purchasing luxury and other restricted items; the delegation will be smaller than last year. The decision comes after more than six months of U.S.-Iran conflict, with no material progress on reopening the Strait of Hormuz or restarting Iran nuclear talks. As host of the UN, the U.S. is constrained by host-country obligations and has previously issued limited visas and activity curbs for representatives from conflict parties. The approval follows a separate U.S. visa denial for Palestinian President Mahmoud Abbas one day earlier.

### 2026-09-18 02:27:54  #國際

【中方強調維護伊核問題政治解決大局】當地時間9月17日上午，在聯合國安理會會議上，美國等國無視涉及伊朗核問題的第2231號決議已於2025年10月18日到期終止，安理會已終止審議伊朗核問題，在安理會強推恢復對伊朗制裁、提出明顯具有爭議的決議草案。中國和俄羅斯在對該草案的表決中投下反對票，巴基斯坦和索馬利亞投下棄權票，草案未獲透過。 中國常駐聯合國代表傅聰大使在表決後的解釋性發言中表示，中方從捍衛公道正義、維護中東地區和平穩定出發，著眼伊朗核問題政治外交解決大局，對決議草案投了反對票。 傅聰大使指出，形勢越是動盪，安理會越要展現領導力，以負責任態度採取行動，避免雙重標準、政治操弄、施壓對抗。 傅聰大使強調，軍事手段和制裁施壓無助於解決問題，只會加劇緊張，升級局勢不符合任何一方利益，平等對話談判才是唯一正途。 據瞭解，這一被否決的決議草案尋求將根據安理會第1929號決議（2010年）設立、將於9月26日屆滿的伊朗制裁委專家小組任期延長一年。該專家小組負責監督對伊朗制裁制度的執行情況。（CCTV國際時訊）

【摩根大通：根本不知道伊朗戰爭何時結束，經濟紅線已現】摩根大通石油分析師稱，“我們根本不知道” 伊朗戰爭何時結束  ，戰爭結束道路越來越難以預測。該行此前認為美國政府不會跨越的多條經濟紅線均已出現，包括油價突破每桶100美元、汽油價格接近每加侖5美元以及國債收益率飆升，令退出策略更不明朗。分析師娜塔莎·卡內瓦等人指出，油價目前接近每桶106美元，而9月原油合理價格估計約為每桶90美元，意味著市場正計入額外400萬桶/日的供應損失風險，此前已造成每日1000萬桶供應中斷。摩根大通稱，包括沙特東-西向輸油管道在內的關鍵能源基礎設施最近遭攻擊，加劇供應進一步緊張擔憂，市場情緒緊張。分析師認為，美國和伊朗均未發出準備緩和局勢的明確訊號，因此認為當前混亂只是暫時的假設越來越難以維持。

Lumber Falls to Nine-Month Low — Lumber futures fell below $550 per thousand board feet, reaching their lowest level in nine months, as elevated borrowing costs continued to weigh on demand. The US housing market remains tight, with mortgage rates near their highest level in over a year and showing no sign of easing after the Federal Reserve hiked its key policy rate, weighing on demand from prospective homebuyers. Building permits also fell 2.7% over the month, although a 7.6% increase in lumber-intensive single-family housing starts in August. Provided an offsetting pressure, US-Canada trade talks fell through, raising concerns over lumber supply. Ottawa had announced counter-tariffs of 25% on US lumber and 50% on plywood following US tariffs on several lumber-related goods. US forestry groups have long accused Canada of distorting the market, noting that 94% of Canadian forestland is publicly owned, compared with 58% privately owned in the US, allowing Canadian lumber to be sold at lower prices.

European Stocks Extend Rebound — European stocks closed firmly higher on Thursday, tracking the positive momentum for stocks worldwide after a pullback in oil and gas prices softened the elevated level for sovereign yields. The Euro STOXX 50 gained 0.9% to 6,323 and the STOXX Europe 600 rose 0.9% to 643. Natural gas benchmarks in the continent and wholesale fuel costs eased after Saudi Arabia signaled its East-West pipeline could return to operation shortly, softening risks of even more aggressive inflation in the Eurozone. Meanwhile, the Fed raised its rates by 25bps, as expected, but the hawkish tone in Chairman Warsh's remarks flattened yield curves across economies with exposure to US credit, also softening long borrowing costs. Banks closed higher, with Santander, BBVA, and Deutsche Bank adding close to 2%. Power-hungry industrials also gained, with Siemens Energy, Schneider, and Siemens jumping between 3.5% and 2.5%.

【奈及利亞翁多州疑似甲醇中毒事件死亡人數升至48人】奈及利亞翁多州政府17日稱，該州居民飲用當地釀造的酒精飲料後疑似中毒事件持續蔓延，死亡人數已升至48人。 翁多州政府稱，該州兩個社群9月初接連出現居民飲用當地釀造的酒精飲料後就醫的情況，10日已報告至少20人死亡。此後事件持續蔓延，截至目前，當地醫療機構已報告超過182例相關病例，其中48人死亡。 當地訊息人士稱，一名本地釀酒者將這種酒精飲料出售給當地居民，其中大多數為年輕人。 當地政府稱，受害者的尿液樣本檢測結果顯示，此次事件疑似與攝入甲醇中毒有關。截至目前，警方已逮捕15名涉嫌生產、銷售該酒精飲料的人員。 當地專家警告稱，甲醇是一種易燃且有毒的醇類物質，不可飲用，人體攝入後可能導致中樞神經系統抑制、永久性失明或死亡。(新華社)

### 2026-09-18 00:40:23  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Palladium Holds Steady — Palladium futures held near $1,300 per ounce, their lowest level in over a month, following the Federal Reserve’s monetary policy meeting. As widely expected, the Fed hiked its benchmark rate for the first time since 2023 and is expected to deliver another hike this year amid elevated economic and geopolitical uncertainty, raising the opportunity cost of holding non-yielding assets such as palladium. Meanwhile, UBS forecasts a market surplus for palladium, as increased recycling and weaker consumption of catalytic converters are expected to more than offset lower mining output compared with last year. Persistent supply vulnerabilities in South Africa, linked to elevated operating costs and power grid instability, continued to raise concerns over mined output, while ongoing trade and geopolitical risks affecting Russian palladium also provided a supportive backdrop for prices.

### 2026-09-18 00:38:39  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #Brent #Impact bullish #WTI #XAUUSD

【Yaroslavl refinery halts crude processing after Ukrainian drone strike】Four industry sources said Slavneft’s Yaroslavl refinery (YANOS) halted crude processing on Thursday after a Ukrainian drone strike damaged equipment. Yaroslavl region governor Mikhail Yevrayev said authorities had foiled a large-scale drone attack: "65 drones were shot down, no casualties. The refinery was damaged and firefighting is ongoing." President Zelenskiy said Ukraine struck the refinery overnight. The plant is about 250 km (155 miles) northeast of Moscow. Sources said the strike damaged the main crude distillation unit AVT-3, with design throughput of 17,140 tonnes/day, roughly 40% of the refinery’s capacity. The refinery’s other major units are AT-4 (11,430 t/d, 27%) and AVT-4 (14,300 t/d, 33%); AVT-4 was already damaged in an Aug. 28 drone attack and remains under repair. Market participants said fuels from the Yaroslavl plant were not listed on the Russian domestic commodity exchange on Thursday.
